The Southeast Anatolia Project is a regional development project that is critical to the Republic of Turkey and the world, with its broad scope, ambitious goals and goals, positive and negative impacts, nearly half a century of history, different technical, political, economic, social, cultural and ecological dimensions. In early stages, the project, covering 22 dams, 19 hydropower plants and various irrigation investments to develop the water and land resources of the Southeast Anatolian region, was transformed into a multi- sectoral, sustainable, human development-based regional development project. Today's main objective is to assess the resources of the Southeast Anatolian region and improve the income levels and quality of life in the region, as well as to improve the differences between the region and other regions.
